<script src="https://ajax.googleapis.com/ajax/libs/angularjs/1.6.9/angular.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://ajax.googleapis.com/ajax/libs/angularjs/1.6.9/angular-sanitize.js"></script>
<script>
    var Rebuy = angular.module('Rebuy', ['ngSanitize']).config(function($interpolateProvider) {
        // Change Angular output syntax so it doesn't conflict with Shopify liquid syntax
        $interpolateProvider.startSymbol('[{').endSymbol('}]');
    });

    Rebuy.controller('RebuyCartRecommendations', function($scope, $http, $timeout) {
        $scope.config = {
            api_key: 'gHWOKKxJjek97depFCuBVEIVNIZWvB7XpP3G0zxddLkJejTqaTDjXH0kM5zvV/AwUuRhGR8SsbFWU2uJz5vtYA==',
            product_ids: '',
            product_limit: '1'
        }

        $scope.language = {
            title: 'Your order goes great with',
            button_label: 'Add to Order',
            sold_out_label: 'Sold Out'
        }

        $scope.current_product = null;
        $scope.recommended_products = null;

        $scope.getProducts = function() {
            $http.get('https://rebuy.abovemarket.com/api/products/recommended', {
                params: {
                    key: $scope.config.api_key,
                    shopify_product_ids: $scope.config.product_ids,
                    limit: $scope.config.product_limit
                }
            })
                .then(function(response){
                    $scope.current_product = response.data.input_product;
                    $scope.recommended_products = response.data.recommended;
                },function(response){
                    console.log('Error!!!');
                    console.log(response.data);
                });
        }

        $scope.formatMoney = function(cents, format) {
            if (typeof cents == 'string') { cents = cents.replace('.',''); }

            var value = '';
            var placeholderRegex = /\{\{\s*(\w+)\s*\}\}/;
            var formatString = (format || '${{amount}}');

            function defaultOption(opt, def) {
                return (typeof opt == 'undefined' ? def : opt);
            }

            function formatWithDelimiters(number, precision, thousands, decimal) {
                precision = defaultOption(precision, 2);
                thousands = defaultOption(thousands, ',');
                decimal   = defaultOption(decimal, '.');

                if (isNaN(number) || number == null) { return 0; }

                number = (number/100.0).toFixed(precision);

                var parts   = number.split('.'),
                    dollars = parts[0].replace(/(\d)(?=(\d\d\d)+(?!\d))/g, '$1' + thousands),
                    cents   = parts[1] ? (decimal + parts[1]) : '';

                return dollars + cents;
            }

            switch(formatString.match(placeholderRegex)[1]) {
                case 'amount':
                    value = formatWithDelimiters(cents, 2);
                    break;
                case 'amount_no_decimals':
                    value = formatWithDelimiters(cents, 0);
                    break;
                case 'amount_with_comma_separator':
                    value = formatWithDelimiters(cents, 2, '.', ',');
                    break;
                case 'amount_no_decimals_with_comma_separator':
                    value = formatWithDelimiters(cents, 0, '.', ',');
                    break;
            }

            return formatString.replace(placeholderRegex, value);
        }

        $scope.sizeImage = function(src, size) {
            var extension = src.split('.').pop();
            size = (typeof size == 'undefined') ? '' : '_' + size;
            return src.replace('.' + extension, size + '.' + extension);
        }

        $scope.featuredImage = function(product, size) {
            var src = '';

            // If product image is set
            if (product.image) {
                src = product.image.src
            }

            // Return sized image
            return $scope.sizeImage(src, size);
        }

        $scope.variantImage = function(product, variant, size) {
            var src = '';

            // If product image is set
            if (product.image) {
                src = product.image.src
            }

            // If variant image id is set
            if (variant.image_id) {
                for (var i = 0; i < product.images.length; i++) {
                    if (product.images[i].id === variant.image_id) {
                        src = product.images[i].src;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }

            // Return sized image
            return $scope.sizeImage(src, size);
        }

        $scope.showVariantSelect = function(product) {
            var show_select = true;

            if (product.variants.length == 1) {
                for (var i = 0; i < product.variants.length; i++) {
                    if (product.variants[i].title.toLowerCase() == 'default title') {
                        show_select = false;
                    }
                }
            }

            return show_select;
        }

        $scope.onSale = function(variant) {
            return (variant.compare_at_price && variant.compare_at_price > variant.price);
        }

        $scope.addToCart = function(product) {
            // Record user action
            $.ajax({
                method: 'POST',
                url: 'https://rebuy.abovemarket.com/api/analytics/add_to_cart',
                data: {
                    key: $scope.config.api_key,
                    shopify_product_id: product.id,
                    shopify_variant_id: product.selected_variant.id,
                    qty: 1,
                    
                },
                crossDomain: true,
                success: function(response) {},
                error: function(response) {
                    console.log('CrossSell.addToCart() ERROR!');
                    console.log(response);
                }
            });

            // Add item to cart
            $http.post('/cart/add.js', {
                id: product.selected_variant.id,
                quantity: 1
            })
                .then(function(response) {
                    window.location = '/cart';
                },function(response){
                    console.log('Error - Add to Cart!!!');
                    console.log(response);
                });
        }

        $scope.learnMore = function(product) {
            $.ajax({
                method: 'POST',
                url: 'https://rebuy.abovemarket.com/api/analytics/view_product',
                data: {
                    key: $scope.config.api_key,
                    shopify_product_id: product.id,
                    shopify_variant_id: product.selected_variant.id,
                    qty: 1,
                    
                },
                crossDomain: true,
                success: function(response) {
                    window.location = '/products/' + product.handle;
                },
                error: function(response) {
                    window.location = '/products/' + product.handle;
                }
            });
        }

        $scope.outOfStock = function(variant) {
            return (variant.inventory_management == 'shopify' && variant.inventory_policy == 'deny' && variant.inventory_quantity <= 0);
        }

        $scope.variantAvailable = function(variant) {
            var available = true;

            if ($scope.outOfStock(variant)) {
                available = false;
            }

            return available;
        }

        $scope.initProduct = function(product) {
            var variant = product.variants[0];

            for (var i = 0; i < product.variants.length; i++) {
                if ($scope.variantAvailable(product.variants[i])) {
                    variant = product.variants[i];
                    break;
                }
            }

            product.selected_variant = variant;
            product.selected_variant_id = variant.id;
        }
        $scope.selectVariant = function(product) {
            for (var i = 0; i < product.variants.length; i++) {
                if(product.variants[i].id == product.selected_variant_id) {
                    product.selected_variant = product.variants[i];
                }
            }
        }

        $scope.init = function() {
            if ($scope.config.api_key) {
                $scope.getProducts();
            } else {
                console.log('Rebuy cross-sell was not initialized because no API Key was provided. You can learn more about API Keys and generate a new one here: https://rebuy.abovemarket.com/api');
            }
        }
    });
</script>

  var isRelevantCartUpdate = function(settings) {
    function dataContains(term) {
      return (
        settings.data &&
        settings.data.indexOf &&
        settings.data.indexOf(term) > -1
      );
    }

    return (
      (settings.url == "/cart/add.js" || settings.url == "/cart/update.js") &&
      (dataContains("attributes[prod") ||
        (dataContains("id=") && dataContains("quantity=")))
    );
  };

  var mobileNav = $(".c-what-is--sidebar-container");

  var mobileNavSticky = mobileNav.offset();

  var scrollFunction = function() {
    if (window.innerWidth < 900) {
      if (window.pageYOffset + 80 > mobileNavSticky.top) {
        mobileNav.css("position", "fixed");
        mobileNav.css("top", "76px");
      } else {
        mobileNav.css("position", "relative");
        mobileNav.css("top", "0px");
      }
    }
  };

  window.onscroll = function() {
    headerSticky();
  };
  var header = $(".c-header");

  var sticky = header.offset();

  function headerSticky() {
    if (window.pageYOffset > sticky.top) {
      header.addClass("sticky");
      $(".c-fs-bar").css("margin-bottom", "80px");
    } else {
      header.removeClass("sticky");
      $(".c-fs-bar").css("margin-bottom", "0px");
    }
  }

  $(".c-mobile--nav-shop").click(function() {
    if ($(this).attr("data-status") === "up") {
      $(".c-mobile--nav-shop-down").css("display", "none");
      $(".c-mobile--nav-shop-up").css("display", "block");
      $(".c-mobile--nav-products").css("height", "600px");

      $(this).attr("data-status", "down");
    } else {
      $(".c-mobile--nav-shop-up").css("display", "none");
      $(".c-mobile--nav-shop-down").css("display", "block");
      $(".c-mobile--nav-products").css("height", "0px");

      $(this).attr("data-status", "up");
    }
  });

  // Closes Cart
  $(".slideout-cart-cover").click(function() {

            </h2>
<p class="c-slides--description-comment">

                  add protein and nutrients</b> to help me get back out there faster.
              Their chicken is my favorite flavor.
            </p>
</div>

<div class="c-slides--description-container">
<h1 class="c-slides--description-heading">Melissa Hartwig</h1>
<h2 class="c-slides--description-sub-heading">
              Creator of Whole30, 4x NY Times Bestseller
            </h2>
<p class="c-slides--description-comment">
              Not all bone broth is created equal, which is why I like Kettle &amp;
              Fire for my Whole30 community. I like how their broth is
              <b>made with bones from grass-fed cattle</b> and slow roasted so
              all the micronutrients make it into the broth.
            </p>
</div>

<div class="c-slides--description-container">
<h1 class="c-slides--description-heading">Mark Sisson</h1>
<h2 class="c-slides--description-sub-heading">
              Creator of Primal Kitchen, NY Times Best Seller (The Keto Reset
              Diet)
            </h2>
<p class="c-slides--description-comment">
              I’m a fan of Kettle &amp; Fire’s Bone Broth for several reasons.
              First, the time (and money) it saves me. I don’t have to spend
              hours turning bones into more digestible gelatin, and I don’t have
              to hunt for a <b>reliable source of grass-fed bones</b> at a cost of $10
              a pound or more.
            </p>
</div>
</div><div aria-describedby="slick-slide-control12" aria-hidden="true" class="c-slides slick-slide" data-slick-index="2" id="slick-slide12" role="tabpanel" style="width: 722px;" tabindex="-1">

</div>
<div class="c-slides--description-container">
<h1 class="c-slides--description-heading">Dr. Cate Shanahan</h1>
<h2 class="c-slides--description-sub-heading">
              Nutritional Consultant for Los Angeles Lakers
            </h2>
<p class="c-slides--description-comment">
<b>I'm always recommending bone broth to my patients and to the
                  athletes I work with</b>. But it's so time consuming to make that few
              people can get enough to meet their nutritional needs. I'm so glad
              that Kettle &amp; Fire is now making bone broth available nationwide,
              and I'm even happier it tastes so good!
            </p>
</div>

<section class="c-new-faq">
<div class="new-faq--container">
<div class="c-faq--header">Frequently Asked Questions</div>
<div class="c-faq--body">
<div class="c-faq--question">
<div class="c-faq--question-header">
<h4>Why do people drink bone broth?</h4>
<div class="c-faq--status-close"></div>
</div>
<div class="c-faq--question-body">
<p>For all sorts of reasons.</p>
<p>
            First, it's delicious to sip from a mug. Second, our customers tell
            us that adding bone broth to their favorite recipes (soups, stews,
            even rice dishes) makes them taste even better than before.
          </p>
<p>
            But we think the #1 reason people drink bone broth is that it's the
            definitive whole food source of collagen. When we slow-simmer our
            grass-fed bones, you get not just collagen, but ALL the supporting
            nutrients that nature surrounds collagen with. That's a big deal.
            Studies have shown that the human body absorbs nutrients more fully
            as whole foods than as isolated supplements.
          </p>
<p>
            And so, even as new collagen pills, powders, and peptides are
            released all the time, bone broth is only becoming more popular.
            Grocery stores that didn't stock it 6 months ago (or only sold 1-2
            brands) have entire shelves now.
          </p>
<p>If you're going to take collagen, why settle?</p>
<p>
            Think of your daily cup of bone broth as putting full-spectrum
            collagen in your body, as opposed to just that one nutrient.
          </p>
</div>
</div>
<!-- next -->
<div class="c-faq--question">
<div class="c-faq--question-header">
<h4>OK, but what makes your bone broth different from the broths I see at the grocery store for $1.99?</h4>
<div class="c-faq--status-close"></div>
</div>
<div class="c-faq--question-body">
<p>Quality and time. </p>
<p>The bones often used for bone broth are whatever random bones a farm will sell for cheap (AKA “carcass bones.”) </p>
<p>The problem with carcass bones is they contain inconsistent amounts of collagen. To make matters worse, these inferior bones are often simmered for just 2-3 hours. If you’ve ever had a weak or pale-looking broth, this is likely why.</p>
<p>In our opinion, bone quality is the #1 corner that’s cut when ANYONE makes bone broth. And even though not all consumers are aware of that, we are.</p>
<p>That’s why we ran systematic collagen yield tests on different kinds of bones. (So. Many. Tests.) And once we found the specific bones that create the most flavorful, collagen-filled broth on a consistent basis...</p>
<p>100% grass-fed-and-finished...</p>
<p>We started cooking exclusively with those.</p>
<p>Even though it costs us around 20% more for bones than other companies. And even though we had to build a literal bone supply chain to serve our growing customer base.</p>
<p>We couldn’t bear to settle.</p>
<p>This is why Kettle &amp; Fire bone broth costs more than other brands. Try a carton and tell us if you can feel (and taste) where that extra money went.</p>
<p>(Hint: There’s a reason we’re the only brand with over 15,000 five-star reviews!)</p>
</div>
</div>
<!-- next -->
<div class="c-faq--question">
<div class="c-faq--question-header">
<h4>How long does it take for the effects of bone broth to "kick in" and become noticeable? </h4>
<div class="c-faq--status-close"></div>
</div>
<div class="c-faq--question-body">
<p>Every individual is different. Therefore, one person's experience may not match your own. And bone broth is not intended to treat, diagnose, or cure any health conditions.</p>
<p>That said, our customers seem to agree that 1 month of daily bone broth consumption is an appropriate amount of time to allow before assessing the results.</p>
<p>Feeling impatient? On the plus side, bone broth tastes amazing from the very first sip!</p>
</div>
</div>
<!-- next -->
<div class="c-faq--question">
<div class="c-faq--question-header">
<h4>Do all of your soups contain bone broth, too?</h4>
<div class="c-faq--status-close"></div>
</div>
<div class="c-faq--question-body">
<p>YES!</p>
<p>In fact, each carton of our soups contain a full serving (roughly 1 mug) of bone broth in addition to the vegetable and / or meat base. We wanted to create soups that infuse the flavors we know and love with the whole food collagen and savory taste of bone broth.</p>
<p>Many of our customers who sip bone broth by itself also eat our soups a 2-3 times per week as a fun, change-of-pace way to get even more broth into their diets.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</section>
<script>
  $(document).ready(function() {
    $(".c-faq--question-header").click(function() {
      var arrow = $(this).find("div");
      var prevArrow = $(".c-faq--question-header-selected").find("div");

      $(".c-faq--question-header-selected").removeClass(
        "c-faq--question-header-selected"
      );
      if (arrow.hasClass("c-faq--status-close")) {
        arrow.removeClass("c-faq--status-close");
        arrow.addClass("c-faq--status-open");
        $(this).next()[0].style.maxHeight =
          $(this).next()[0].scrollHeight + "px";
        $(this).addClass("c-faq--question-header-selected");
      } else {
        arrow.removeClass("c-faq--status-open");
        arrow.addClass("c-faq--status-close");
        $(this).next()[0].style.maxHeight = null;
        $(this).removeClass("c-faq--question-header-selected");
      }
      prevArrow.removeClass("c-faq--status-open");
      prevArrow.addClass("c-faq--status-close");
    });
  });
</script>

<script>
//Talkable auto apply coupon
 window._talkableq.push(['gleam_reward', {
    callback: function(coupon) {
      if (window.jQuery) {
        $('body').on('submit', "form[action='/cart']", function(data) {
          $('<input />').attr('type', 'hidden')
            .attr('name', 'discount')
            .attr('value', coupon)
            .appendTo($("form[action='/cart']"));
          return true;
        });
      } else {
        var forms = document.getElementsByTagName('form'),
            discount = document.createElement('input');
        discount.type = 'hidden';
        discount.name = 'discount';
        discount.value = coupon;
        for (var i = 0; i < forms.length; i++) {
          if (forms[i].action.indexOf('/cart') !== -1) {
            forms[i].appendChild(discount);
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }]);
</script>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
  var setCookie = function (name, value, days) {
      var expires = "";
      if (days) {
        var date = new Date();
        date.setTime(date.getTime() + (days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
        expires = "; expires=" + date.toUTCString();
      }
      document.cookie = name + "=" + (value || "") + expires + "; path=/";
    }

  var getCookie = function (name) {
      var nameEQ = name + "=";
      var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
      for (var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
        var c = ca[i];
        while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') c = c.substring(1, c.length);
        if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) == 0) return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
      }
      return null;
      }

  var notBYB = function(){
    $('.byb-btn').attr('href', '/collections/products/?view=oneTime')
  }

  if(window.location.href.includes('reconfirm')) {
    setCookie('reconfirm', true, 1)
  }


  if(window.location.href.includes('freep1')) {
    setCookie('freep1', true, 1)
  }
  if(window.location.href.includes('freep2')) {
    setCookie('freep2', true, 1)
    //console.log('set chicken cookie')
  }
  if(window.location.href.includes('freep3')) {
    setCookie('freep3', true, 1)
  }

  if(window.location.href.includes('addOn')){
    setCookie('addOn', true, 1)
  }

  if(window.location.href.includes('oneTime')){
    if(getCookie('oneTime')) {
      notBYB()
    } else {
      notBYB()
      setCookie('oneTime', true, 1)
    }
  } else {
    if(getCookie('oneTime')){
      notBYB()
    } else {

    }
  }

 
  var lazyloadImages = document.querySelectorAll("img.lazy");    
  var lazyloadThrottleTimeout;
  
 
  function lazyload () {
    if(lazyloadThrottleTimeout) {
      clearTimeout(lazyloadThrottleTimeout);
    }    
    
    lazyloadThrottleTimeout = setTimeout(function() {
        var scrollTop = window.pageYOffset;
        lazyloadImages.forEach(function(img) {
            if(img.offsetTop < (window.innerHeight + scrollTop)) {
              img.src = img.dataset.src;
              img.classList.remove('lazy');
            }
        });
        if(lazyloadImages.length == 0) { 
          document.removeEventListener("scroll", lazyload);
          window.removeEventListener("resize", lazyload);
          window.removeEventListener("orientationChange", lazyload);
        }
    }, 20);
  }
  
  document.addEventListener("scroll", lazyload);
  window.addEventListener("resize", lazyload);
  window.addEventListener("orientationChange", lazyload);
});
</script>
